## §1 重要提示

### §1 Important Notice

1.1 本公司董事会及其董事保证本报告所载资料不存在任何虚假记载、误导性陈述或者重大遗漏，并对其内容的真实性、准确性和完整性承担个别及连带责任。

1.1 The Board of Directors (BOD) and directors of the Company hereby guarantee that the data in the present report contain no false representation, misleading statements, and serious omissions, and shall be severally and jointly liable for the authenticity, accuracy, and completeness of the content.

1.2 本公司董事长史跃峰、董事会秘书李建卫、监事长王新光、副行长（主持工作）王宗华、副行长陈瑞源、周世国、仪修喜、朱毅达、会计机构负责人吴玉田保证年度报告中财务报告的真实、完整。

1.2 Chairman Shi Yuefeng, Secretary Li Jianwei, Chief Supervisor Wang Xinguang, Vice President (managing president) Wang Zonghua, Vice President Chen Ruiyuan, Zhou Shiguo, Yi Xiuxi, Zhu Yida, Head of the accounting department Wu Yutian guarantee that the financial report in the annual report is authentic and complete.

### （二）2010年增资扩股情况

#### (II) Share capital increase

报告期内，本公司没有发生增资扩股，进行了一次红利分配，具体内容如下：

There is no share capital increase in the period. A dividend distribution has been made as the following:

根据本公司2010年股东大会年会确定的2009年度利润分配方案，按15%分配红利，采取分配现金和送股的方式。按1,396,805,528股的15%分配红利209,520,829.20元，其中，分配现金41,903,612.20元，送股167,617,217股。经山东省银监局批准，我行注册资本由1,396,805,528元变更为1564422745元，新增的167,617,217元为我行以送股的方式直接转增资本。

As decided by the Company’s 2010 annual general meeting, profit distribution plan for 2009 defined a proportion of 15% for distributing dividends with cash and gift shares. The dividend calculated based on the shares of 1,396,805,528 and proportion of 15% is RMB 209,520,829.20 Yuan, which include 41,903,612.20 Yuan in cash and 167,617,217 in gift shares. As approved by Shandong Banking Regulatory Bureau, the registered capital of the Bank is increased from 1,396,805,528 Yuan to 1,564,422,745 Yuan with the gift shares of 167,617,217 as directly increased capital.

# 1. Rongdaxin Industries Development Co., Ltd.

融达信实业发展有限公司持有本公司240，502，080股，占总股本的17.22%，法人代表王燕，注册资金3亿元，是一家集技、工、贸于一体的高科技企业，致力于存储业的发展研究，已在国内建立起多家OEM客户，公司凭借领先的技术、可靠的产品、完善的服务，与国内的金融、医疗、邮政、交通、教学以及众多中小型企业建立起广泛的合作关系。

Rongdaxin Industries Development Co., Ltd. owns 240, 502, 080 shares of the Company, or 17.22% of the total share capital. Its legal representative is Wang Yan. With registered capital of 300 million Yuan, it is a high-tech enterprise integrating technological, industrial, and trading businesses. Supported by its R&D activities in memory industry, it has established several OEMs in China. It has also established extensive partnerships with many small and medium businesses in China's financial, medical, postal, traffic, and educational industries through its leading technologies, reliable products and quality services.

上海德莱科技股份有限公司持有本公司128，693，929万股，占总股本的9.21%，法人代表尹一凡，注册资金25,000万元，主要从事证券、银行、基金、交通等行业应用软件的开发与销售，开发、销售中西文字符图形终端、网络终端、金融机具、通信产品、机电一体化产品及成套商用电子设备、税控系列产品、软件及系统集成、打印机等。

## 6.4 员工情况

### 6.4 Employees

报告期末，本公司在职员工1，337人，其中：高级职称26人，中级职称439人，初级职称90人。中层以上管理人员135人，占全行员工的10.09%。大学本科以上学历672人，占比50.3%；专科学历460人，占比34.4%；中专以下学历205人，占比15.3%。全行共有内退员工137人。

As at the end of the reporting period, the Company employs 1,337 active employees, in whom 26 of them have senior qualifications, 439 have medium qualifications, and 90 have primary qualifications. Medium level managers of 135 account for 10.09% of our staff. In terms of educational background, 672 employees or 50.3% are graduates from universities, 460 or 34.4% are graduates from colleges, and 205 or 15.3% are those from schools below the technical school level. The Bank now has 137 employees retired early.

## §7 公司治理结构

## §7 Company Governance Structure

## 7.1 公司治理情况

### 7.1 Company Governance

本公司自成立以来即按照《中华人民共和国公司法》和《中华人民共和国商业银行法》的有关规定制定了公司章程及其配套的管理制度,确立了股东大会、董事会、监事会和经营层的职责分工，形成较为完善的公司治理结构。

Since its inception, as per related provisions in the “Corporate Law of the People’s Republic of China” and “Commercial Bank Law of the People’s Republic of China”, the Company has established an appropriated company governance structure by defined Articles of Association and its supporting management systems as well as defined roles and responsibilities of the general meeting, Board of Directors, Board of Supervisors, and the management.

本公司无控股股东，在人员、资产、财务上完全独立。

Without any controlling shareholder, the Company is very independent in terms of staff, assets, and finance.

报告期内，公司治理具体情况如下：

Details of company governance in the reporting period:

### （一）关于股东和股东大会

### (Ⅰ) About shareholders and the general meeting

本公司具有较为合理的股权结构和运行规范，确保了所有股东享有平等地位并能够充分行使自己的权利。本公司严格按照《公司法》的规定召集、召开股东大会，保证股东参加会议并行使质询权和表决权。

The appropriate equity structure and operating procedures established ensure that any shareholder can exercise his/her rights fairly and fully. Shareholders’ rights in participating in the general meeting, enquiring, and voting are safeguarded with the general meetings convened and held in strict compliance with the “Corporate Law”.

### （二）关于董事、董事会和专门委员会

### (II) About Directors, Board of Directors, and Special Committees

战略委员会成员为：史跃峰董事长（主任）、副主任委员杨德勇、委员由王宗华董事、李建卫董秘、战略研究部总经理、计划财务部总经理组成。

## （三）关于监事和监事会

### (III) About Supervisors and the Board of Supervisors

本公司监事的任职资格和选聘程序、监事会的人数和人员构成均符合公司章程规定的要求。本公司监事会严格对本公司进行财务、授信检查和监督。本公司监事会能够按照公司章程的规定认真履行职责，本着对股东高度负责的精神，对公司财务、董事长、董事、行长、其他高级管理人员履行职责的合法合规性进行监督。

Qualifications and selection procedures of the Company’s Supervisors as well as members’ number and their structure for the Board of Supervisors comply with the Articles of Association. The Board of Supervisors is responsible for inspecting and monitoring the financial and credit position of the Company. In faithful performing of its roles and responsibilities as per the Articles of Association, in particular, in improving its accountability for the shareholders, the Board of Supervisors monitors the performing of responsibilities by Financial Officer, Chairman, Directors, Presidents, and other Officers to ensure such

performance complies with related laws and regulations.

（四）关于信息披露与透明度

(IV) About Information Disclosure and Transparency

本公司根据《商业银行信息披露暂行办法》和监管部门的相关规定，制定了《潍坊银行信息披露暂行办法》，并按照监管规定于2010年度进行信息披露，在国家级报刊《金融时报》披露2009年年度报告摘要，制作了《潍坊银行2009年年度报告》单行本，供股东及客户查阅。本公司认真对待股东的来信、来电、来访和咨询，确保所有股东有平等的机会获得信息，切实维护广大股东的利益。

As per related provisions in the “Provisional Practices in Information Disclosure by Commercial Banks” and regulators, the Company established the “Provisional Practices in Information Disclosure by the Bank of Weifang”. The Company disclosed its Summary of the 2009 Annual Report in national grade newspaper “Financial Times” and prepared booklet “2009 Annual Report of Bank of Weifang” in 2010 as per regulatory provisions. Shareholders and clients can get information through the Report. The Company takes care of the letters, telephone calls, visits and enquiries from shareholders to ensure that every shareholder has fair chance to obtain information to safeguard their interests.

### 7.2 经营决策体系

## 7.2 Decision-making system for operation

本公司最高权力机构是股东大会,通过董事会进行决策、管理，由监事会行使监督职能。行长受聘于董事会，对本公司日常经营管理全面负责。本公司实行一级法人体制，各分支机构均为非独立核算单位，其经营管理活动根据总部授权进行，并对总部负责。

General meetings of the Company exercise the highest authority. The Board of Directors is responsible for decision-making and management while the Board of Supervisors is responsible for monitoring these processes. Appointed by the BOD, the President is totally responsible for daily operation and management of the Company. With the arrangement of single corporate, no branch is independent in accounting. Operating and managing activities in the branches are carried out as per the Company's authorization. These branches shall report to the Company.

## 7.3 高级管理人员考评及激励机制

## 7.3 Assessment and motivation of officers

报告期内,本公司是按《潍坊银行董事会对高级管理层绩效评价试行办法》对高级管理人员进行年度考评,制订了《潍坊银行独立董事、外部监事管理暂行办法》，并按照董事会批准的《潍坊

银行董事、监事薪酬管理办法》、《潍坊银行总部2010年行级领导及董秘、行助、首席风险官薪酬管理办法》进行薪酬发放。

In the reporting period, the Company carried out annual assessment of officers as per the “Provisional Practice for Evaluating Management Performance by BOD of the Bank of Weifang”. The compensation will be paid as per the “Provisional Practice for Managing Independent Directors and External Supervisors by the Bank of Weifang” prepared, “Compensation Management for Directors and Supervisors of the Bank of Weifang” approved by the BOD, and “Compensation Management for Presidents, BOD Secretary, Administrative Assistant, and Chief Risk Officer in 2010 in Headquarters of the Bank of Weifang”.

### §8 General Meetings

## 8.1 召开股东大会情况

## 8.1 General meetings held

本公司 2010 年共召开二次股东大会，分别为潍坊银行 2010 年股东大会年会及 2010 年第一次临时股东大会，会议召开情况如下：

Two general meetings were held in 2010. There are the 2010 annual general meeting of the bank of Weifang and the first extraordinary general meeting in 2010. Details of the meetings are as the following:

潍坊银行 2010 年股东大会年会于 2010 年 4 月 17 日在潍坊鸢飞大酒店召开，参加会议的股东和股东代表 100 人，代表股份 129512 万股，占股份总额 139681 万股的 92.72%。会议方式、人数符合《潍坊银行章程》规定比例。史跃峰董事长、秦学昌董事、陈学俭董事因公请假，分别委托王宗华董事、杨德勇董事、陈瑞源董事行使董事权利，杨学亭监事因公请假。潍坊银监分局鞠法明同志、潍坊银行高级管理层成员列席本次会议，山东求是和信律师事务所王玫、张彩云律师见证本次会议。

The 2010 annual general meeting of the Bank of Weifang was held in Weifang Yuanfei Grand Hotel on April 17, 2010. 100 shareholders and shareholder representatives, representing 1,295,120,000 shares, or 92.72% of the total of 1,396,810,000 shares, participated in the meeting. The conferencing and attendance of the meeting complies with quorum specified by “Articles of Association of the Bank of Weifang.”

Weifang". Absence for business reasons, Chairman Shi Yuefeng, Director Qin Xuechang and Director Chen Xuejian entrust Director Wang Zonghua, Director Yang Deyong, and Director Chen Ruiyuan respectively for exercising director's rights. Supervisor Yang Xueting also was absence for business reasons. Ju Faming form Weifang Banking Regulatory Branch Bureau and members of senior management of the Bank of Weifang attended the meeting. Lawyer Wang Mei and lawyer Zhang Caiyun from Shandong Qiushi Hexin Law Firm witnessed the meeting.

潍坊银行 2010 年第一次临时股东大会于 2010 年 10 月 9 日在潍坊鸢飞大酒店召开，参加会议的股东和股东代表 26 人，代表股份 141188 万股，占股份总额 156442 万股的 90.25%。会议方式、人数符合《潍坊银行章程》规定比例。潍坊银监分局鞠法明同志列席本次会议，山东求是和信律师事务所陶书健、王玫律师见证本次会议。

The 2010 annual first extraordinary general meeting of the \textbf{Bank} of Weifang was held in Weifang Yuanfei Grand Hotel on October 9, 2010. 26 shareholders and shareholder representatives, representing 1,411,880,000 shares, or 90.25% of the total of 1,564,420,000 shares, participated in the meeting. The conferencing and attendance of the meeting complies with quorum specified by "Articles of Association of the Bank of Weifang". Ju Faming form Weifang Banking Regulatory Branch Bureau attended the meeting. Lawyer Tao Shujian and lawyer Wang Mei from Shandong Qiushi Hexin Law Firm witnessed the meeting.

### §9 董事会报告

### §9 BOD Report

### 9.1 报告期内整体经营情况

9.1 Overall operational performance in the reporting period

## 一、全行经营指标再创历史最好水平，主要重点工作取得突破性进展

## I. The Bank achieved record high performance in operational indicators and breakthrough in key programs

至 2010 年末，全行总资产突破 400 亿元，达到 403.25 亿元，较年初增长 28.22%；本外币存款余额 348.80 亿元，增长 21.83%；各项贷款余额 229.07 亿元，增长 22.78%。实现净利润 5.72 亿元，增长 63.438%；上缴税金位列全市纳税企业十强。资产质量进一步提高，不良贷款率 1.07%，

不良贷款继续实现“双降”；拨备覆盖率大幅提高，年末拨备覆盖率指标为 222.71%，较年初提高 68.94 个百分点，抵御风险能力明显增强。

By the end of 2010, total assets of the Bank reached up to RMB 40 billion (40.325 billion), increased by 28.22% on the year opening amount; deposit balance in domestic and foreign currencies was RMB 34.88 billion, an increase of 21.83%; loans balance was RMB 22.907 billion, an increase of 22.78%; net profit was RMB 572 million, an increase of 63.438%; and amount of taxes paid was among the top 10 in the City. With decrease of both the rate of non-performance loans (1.07%) and non-performance loans, the capital quality was further improved. The provision coverage as at the end of the year was 222.71%, a substantial increase of 68.94% on the opening, indicated the notably increased capacity in preventing risks.

## 1、业务转型战略持续推进

## 1. Promoting the strategy of business transformation

截止 2010 年末，我行小微业务贷款余额达到 123.81 亿元，小微业务占全行贷款余额超过 50%，小微贷款户数突破万户，小微业务为我行创造了巨大的利润增长和品牌价值。与德国储蓄银行国际基金会合作的微贷中心在人才培养、流程与制度建设方面迈出了重要步伐，营销实现可喜突破，贷款余额已经突破了 3000 万元,基本完成 57 名优秀微贷客户经理的培训上岗，在全市产生重要反响。个人业务工作方面，11 月召开了全行性个人业务工作会议，制订了大力发展个人业务的各项具体措施，提高了全行对个人业务重要性的认识，至 2010 年末，个人储蓄业务实现较快增长，增长额、增幅和占比均排在全省城商行前列。

By the end of 2010, loan balance for small and mini businesses reached to RMB 12.381 billion, accounting for 50% of the total loan balance. With more than 10 thousand users of the small and mini loans, the business have created enormous profit increase and brand value for the Bank. The mini loan center established in cooperation with German Savings Bank Foundation International has facilitated greatly in training talents and building processes and systems. Satisfactory performance of the marketing program brought more than RMB 30 million loan balance in this sector. 57 mini loan customer managers trained and deployed was an important event in the City. The personal business working meeting in November proposed detailed measures for promoting the business development and awareness of its importance. By the end of 2010, personal savings business achieved leading growth among city commercial banks in the province in terms of its increased amount, growth, and proportion.

## 2、跨区域发展战略深入推进

## 2. Promoting the strategy of trans-regional development

一是青岛分行发展局面良好。青岛分行开业以来，坚持立足当地小微业务和青岛市民的市场定位，在总行资源的战略倾斜下，克服开业初期市场认可度低、渠道建设不健全的困难，坚持走差异化的经营方式，取得了较好的成绩。至2010年末，各项存款突破20亿元，贷款达到7.7亿元。其中，小微贷款余额达到5亿元，占总贷款比达到65%。

The Qingdao Branch laid a good foundation for development. Committed to serve local small and mini businesses as well as local residents, the Branch achieved satisfactory results in its differential services by overcoming difficulties from lower market recognition and to be developed marketing channels. By the end of 2010, deposits reached to more than RMB 2 billion and loans of 770 million. The small and mini loans balance was RMB 500 million, accounting for 65% of the total loans.

二是胶南海汇村镇银行业务发展迅速。至年末海汇银行存款达到3.5亿元，贷款达到2.8亿元，实现经营利润1180万元，实现净利润390万元。海汇银行成立以来以廉洁、高效、务实的工作作风作为企业的核心竞争力之一，与当地政府、机关单位的合作关系进一步增强，得到了青岛银监局和当地企业的充分认可。

The Bank also saw rapid development in Jiaonan Haihui village and town banking business. By the end of the year, Haihui has deposits of RMB 350 million and loans of 280 million with operating profit of 11.8 million and net profit of 3.9 million. In further corporation with local governments and governmental organizations, an honest, efficient, and practical Haihui Bank was fully recognized by Qingdao Banking Regulatory Bureau and local businesses.

## 3、县域扩张战略又有新亮点

## 3. New progress in county business expansion

6月18日，青州金鼎支行成立，这标志着我行拉开了县域第二轮扩张的序幕。新设县域第二支行将专注于小微客户与市民服务，与第一批支行实现部分管理资源共享、市场定位区分、团队协作、差异化发展的效果，为我行县域扩张战略取得新的更大成果创造条件。截至2010年底，9家县域支行各项存款余额达到63.81亿元，存款增量占全行存款增量的38.9%；各项贷款余额达到61.13亿元，贷款增量占全行贷款增量的56%。其中小微贷款余额36.43亿元，占全行小微贷款余额的15.08%。实现账面利润达到2.61亿元，同比增长77.39%。县域机构已经成为推动全行发展的主导力量，这标志着我行县域扩张战略在持续深化中基础不断稳固。

<div style="text-align: center;">The establishment of Qingzhou Jinding Branch on June 18 marked the beginning of second round expansion program in the Bank's county business. The second branches in various counties will focus on</div>


small and mini businesses as well as services to local residents. In sharing part of the management resources of the first branches, filling different market niche, team working, and differentiating, these banks provided competitive edges in county business expansion. By the end of 2010, 9 county branches have deposit balance of RMB 6.381 billion, the increase of which accounts for 38.9% of the total increase. Loans balance reached to RMB 6.113 billion, the increase of which accounts for 56% of the total increase. In particular, loan balance of small and mini businesses was RMB 3.643 billion, accounting for 15.08% of the total. Face profit of these branches was RMB 261 million, an increase of 77.39% on YoY basis. County branches have become a major driving force of the Bank's development, providing solder foundation for county development.

## 二、推进改革创新，转变自身增长模式

#### II. Transforming the model of organic growth by reform and innovation

一是推进差异化发展战略。2010年董事会在充分调研的基础上制定了差异化发展战略指导意见，计划用三年左右时间，把潍坊银行打造成主要为小微客户和市民提供综合金融服务、具有显著经营特色、极强创新应变能力和良好品牌形象的优秀股份制商业银行。

The first is promotion of a differentiated development strategy. Based on extensive market research, the BOD proposed in 2010 guidelines for differentiated development strategy, which plans to establish an excellent commercial bank in about three years by focusing on small and mini businesses, providing citizens with comprehensive financial services. The Bank will feature unique operational characteristics, strong innovative and adaptive capacities, and good branding image.

二是营销体制改革迈出实质性步伐。目前已经形成了房地产金融服务中心、汽车金融服务中心、钢材物流金融服务中心、微贷中心以及纺织专业支行、火车站支行等十家特色支行。先期转型定位的十家支行将成为服务全行业务转型、凸显差异化发展理念的排头兵。目前，转型定位支行工作积极性高昂，工作成效正在逐步显现。

The second is implementing marketing system reform. Now the Bank has 10 special branches such as real estate financial service center, auto financial service center, steel logistics financial service center, mini loan center, textile branch, and railway station branch. These pilot branches will provide valuable experiences for transforming and differentiating the whole Bank. Now, these efforts are in the full swing and producing satisfactory results.

三是管理精细化水平不断提高。在 2008-2009 年进位升级工作的基础上，2010 年我行又组织实施以质量管理体系标准化建设为主要内容的全面精细化管理。这项工作的启动将会实现潍坊银

行核心业务的流程标准化、产品标准化和服务标准化。

The third is on systematic management. Based on the management upgrading program in 2008 – 2009, the Bank introduced a total management improving program focused on building a standard quality management system. The program will bring standard core business processes, standard products and services.

四是推进了内控机制的创新。建立了合规主管出帐审核机制和专业化的贷后管理中心，进一步加强了我行的内控管理，增加了授信风险防线。

The fourth is on innovation of internal control. The compliance officer, expenditure items review mechanism, and professional after loan management center will further our internal control and ability in preventing credit risk.

## 三、转变董事会工作方式，进一步提高法人治理水平

#### III. Improving corporate governance by transforming BOD functioning

2010 年董事会将主要精力投入到战略管理和改革创新上来，实现了由审批型工作方式向指导型工作方式的转变。大幅度下放对经营层授权的同时，集中精力研究全行发展的战略问题。今年以来的多项重大改革创新直接由董事会及下属部门推进，组织推进了营销体制改革，加强对全行发展战略重点工作指导督促，出台了人力资源管理、绩效考核、差异化发展、强化内控方面的若干指导意见，进一步提高了公司治理水平。

In 2010, BOD focused on strategic management and reform innovation, providing guidelines rather than approving documents. BOD focused on study development strategies while decentralized authority in daily operation. Several major reforms and innovations were directly promoted by the BOD and its departments, including reform in marketing system, guiding and promoting development strategic programs, guidelines on HR management, performance evaluation, differential development, and internal control for improving corporate governance.

## 四、持续加强团队建设

#### IV. Team work building

一是建立了总行机关效能监督机制。通过效能审计、监事会巡视、民主评议、效能投诉等途径，全面评价行级高管、总行各部门的服务效能，下大气力整治效能低下，服务意识较差、服务质量不高的机关病。

The first is the establishment of monitoring mechanism on the Bank’s departmental performance. In reducing bureaucracies like less-functioning, reluctance with service, and bad service, measures like

performance auditing, BOS inspection, public evaluation, and performance complaints are adopted for a total evaluation of the functioning of senior officers and the Bank’s departments.

二是落实 121 领导班子下基层调研制度。董事会尤其是执行董事能够做到督促和带头深入基层、了解基层，切实为基层排忧解难。

The second is implementing primary research program by 121 leaders. BOD, especially the Executive Director shall play a leading role in site visiting for understanding actual situations there and coming up with practical solutions.

三是做好年内为员工做十件好事、实事的承诺。根据确定的为员工年内办的十件好事实事的承诺，团购住房、提高“五险一金”缴费比例、建立企业年金、为职工体检等十件好事年内基本完成。

The third is demonstration of commitment in improving employees' welfare through 10 programs. As agreed, the 10 programs such as group purchasing of houses, increasing contribution rate of “five insurance and one fund”, establishing annuity, and employee physical examination were completed within the year.

四是注重加强员工培训工作。翠竹计划按季持续推进，培训的覆盖面不断加大，新一轮高层管理人员 EMBA 培训计划按期进行，完成了第二期清华大学 MBA 培训班的培训工作并顺利结业。

The fourth is enhancing employee training. Continue promotion of the Cuizhu program by quarters increased its coverage. The new round of EMBA training was carried out for senior officers as scheduled. The second MBA training session in Tsinghua University was completed successfully.

9.2 报告期内董事会工作情况

## 9.2 BOD's actual work in the reporting period

一是及时召开股东会、董事会及各专门委员会会议，充分发挥董事会的领导和决策作用。一年来，董事会对重大经营事项行使了决策管理权。全体董事认真履行境内外监管机构的有关要求，勤勉尽责，按时出席会议，各董事及独立董事也积极发表专业意见。全年董事会先后组织召开了20次全体会议（其中现场会6次，通讯会14次），审议了涉及全行经营管理各个方面的共94项议题。按时召开了股东大会年会，对董事会（监事会）的年度工作报告、财务预决算报告等提交股东大会进行了审议，并向股东大会通报了董事（监事）的履职情况报告、关联交易情况报告及银监部门对潍坊银行年度监管意见。年内召开了一次临时股东大会，审议了我行增资扩股事项。

<div style="text-align: center;">The first is leading and decisions making through the general meeting, BOD meetings, and special</div>


committee meetings as scheduled. In this year, the BOD made decisions on key operating issues. In meeting related requirements from domestic and foreign regulators; all Directors duly perform their roles and responsibilities and attend meetings as scheduled. Directors and Independent Directors actively proposed professional comments and suggestions. The BOD organized 20 plenary meetings (6 live meetings and 14 tele-meetings) in this year. A total of 94 issues on the Bank's operation and management were reviewed. The general meetings held in time reviewed the annual working report of the BOD (BOS), financial report on budget plan and actual budget, and other documents presented. BOD also presented to the general meetings the performance report of Directors (Supervisors), reports on affiliated transactions, and annual regulatory opinions by banking regulators. The first extraordinary general meeting in the year reviewed the issue on share capital increase.

二是组织完成年度外部审计和信息披露工作。组织外部审计部门对2009年的经营成果进行了审计，确保了年度经营成果的真实性，并对股东大会进行了报告。根据《商业银行信息披露暂行办法》的规定，对我行2009年度财务会计报告、风险管理状况、公司治理状况、年度重大事项等主要内容进行了摘要，并于2009年4月30日在《金融时报》进行公开披露。

The second is annual external audit and information disclosure. BOD entrusted external auditor for auditing 2009 operational results to ensure their authenticity. The audit was presented to the general meeting. Summary of the 2009 annual financial statement, risk control position, corporate governance, and key issues is disclosed in the “Financial Times” on April 30, 2009 as per the provisions of the “Provisional Practices of Disclosing Information by Commercial Banks”.

三是并根据年度分红方案，进行了新股权证的换发工作。

The third is change of new certificate of subscription according to the annual dividend distribution plan.

### 9.3 董事会 2010 年度利润分配方案

## 9.3 BOD plan for 2010 profit distribution

由于目前潍坊银行资本充足率接近监管临界点，暂时不进行利润分配，待增资扩股完成后，潍坊银行将组织临时股东大会审议利润分配事项。

There is no profit distribution for the moment as the Bank’s capital adequacy rate is approaching regulatory limit. After the share capital increase is completed, the Bank will convene extraordinary general meeting for reviewing profit distribution.

### 9.4 董事会 2011 年度经营目标

### 9.4 BOD 2011 annual operational objectives

### (一) 存款指标

## (Ⅰ) Deposit objective

2011 年,各项存款年末余额达到 397.10 亿元,力争突破 400 亿元,时点余额同比增加 51.80 亿元,增长 15%;日均余额同比增加 52.01 亿元,增长 17%。

In 2011, deposits year ending balance shall be RMB 39.71 billion, 40 billion is more preferable. Time point balance shall increase by RMB 5.18 billion, or 15% on YoY basis; daily average balance shall increase by RMB 5.201 billion, or 17% on YoY basis.

对公存款：年末余额达到248.30亿元，时点余额同比增加31.29亿元，增长14.42%；日均余额同比增加35.35亿元，增长18.84%。其中：结算存款年末余额达到163.14亿元，时点余额同比增加12.52亿元，增长8.31%；日均余额同比增加15.91亿元，增长11.62%。保证金存款年末余额达到85.16亿元，时点余额同比增加18.77亿元，增长28.28%；日均余额同比增加19.44亿元，增长38.36%。

Corporate deposits: year ending balance shall be RMB 24.83 billion, time point balance shall increase by RMB 3.129 billion, or 14.42% on YoY basis; daily average balance shall increase by RMB 3.535 billion, or 18.84% on YoY basis. Including: settlement deposits year ending balance shall be RMB 16.341 billion, time point balance shall increase by RMB 1.252 billion, or 8.31% on YoY basis; daily average balance shall increase by RMB 1.591 billion, or 11.62% on YoY basis; and margin deposits year ending balance shall be RMB 8.516 billion, time point balance shall increase by RMB 1.877 billion, or 28.28% on YoY basis; daily average balance shall increase by RMB 1.944 billion, or 38.36% on YoY basis.

储蓄存款：年末余额达到148.80亿元,时点余额同比增加20.51亿元,增长15.99%；日均余额同比增加16.66亿元,增长14.08%。

Saving deposits: year ending balance shall be RMB 14.88 billion, time point balance shall increase by RMB 2.051 billion, or 15.99% on YoY basis; daily average balance shall increase by RMB 1.666 billion, or 14.08% on YoY basis.

## （二）贷款指标

#### (II) Loan objectives

2011年,各项贷款年末余额达到266.60亿元,同比增加34.77亿元,增长15%;

In 2011, loans year ending balance shall be RMB 26.66 billion, increasing by RMB 3.477 billion, or 15% on YoY basis;

纯贷款：2011年末余额达到251.60亿元,同比增加34.88亿元,增长16.09%;其中：公司类客户贷款年末余额135.80亿元,同比增加11.44亿元,增长9.20%;小微类客户贷款年末余额102.65亿元,同比增加17.44亿元,增长20.47%;消费贷款年末余额13.15亿元,同比增加6亿元,增长83.92%;

Pure loans: in 2011, loans year ending balance shall be RMB 25.16 billion, increasing by RMB 3.488 billion, or 16.09% on YoY basis; including: corporate clients' loans year ending balance shall be RMB 13.58 billion, increasing by RMB 1.144 billion, or 9.20% on YoY basis; small and mini clients' loans year ending balance shall be RMB 10.265 billion, increasing by RMB 1.744 billion, or 20.47% on YoY basis; consumption loans year ending balance shall be RMB 1.315 billion, increasing by RMB 600 million, or 83.92% on YoY basis;

贴现贷款：2011年末余额15亿元,同比减少0.11亿元,下降0.71%。

Discount loans: in 2011, year ending balance shall be RMB 1.5 billion, decreasing by RMB 11 million, or 0.71% on YoY basis;

### （三）中间业务收入指标

### (III) Objectives on intermediary business income

2011 年,中间业务收入达到 5,560 万元,在上年基础上增长不低于 30%。其中：一般结算业务手续费收入达到 1,376 万元,同比增加 216 万元,增长 18.62%;国际业务手续费收入达到 1,230 万元,同比增加 410 万元,增长 50%;银行卡业务手续费收入达到 709 万元,同比增加 184 万元,增长 35%;代理保险手续费收入达到 940 万元,同比增加 80 万元,增长 9.3%;财务顾问及业务咨询手续费收入达到 1,245 万元,同比增加 392 万元,增长 46%;证券买卖手续费收入达到 60 万元,与去年基本持平。

In 2011, intermediary business income shall reach to RMB 55.6 million, more than 30% increase from last year, including: fees from general settlement business of over RMB 13.76 million, increasing by RMB 2.16 million, or 18.62% on YoY basis; fees from international business of over RMB 12.3 million, increasing by RMB 4.1 million, or 50% on YoY basis; fees from bank card business of over RMB 7.09 million, increasing by RMB 1.84 million, or 35% on YoY basis; fees from insurance agent service of over RMB 9.4 million, increasing by RMB 0.8 million, or 9.3% on YoY basis; fees from financial advisory and business consulting of over RMB 12.45 million, increasing by RMB 3.92 million, or 46% on YoY basis; fees from securities transaction of more than RMB 0.6 million, same as last year.

## （四）国际业务结算量指标

(IV) Objectives of international business settlement

国际业务结算量达到 13 亿美元，比上年增加 4 亿美元，增长 44%。

International business settlement shall reach to USD 1.3 billion, increasing by USD 400 million, or 44% on YoY basis.

### （五）利润指标

## (V) Objectives of profits

2011 年,实现经营利润 97,089 万元,同比增加 7,746 万元,增长 8.67% (可比口径比较增长 26.4%);实现税后净利润 62,227 万元,同比增加 5,408 万元,增长 9.52% (可比口径比较增长 31.24%)。

In 2011, operating profit shall reach to RMB 970.89 million, increasing by RMB 77.46 million, or 8.67% on YoY basis (increasing by 26.4% on comparable basis); profit after taxes shall reach to RMB 622.27 million, increasing by RMB 54.08 million, or 9.52% on YoY basis (increasing by 31.24% on comparable basis);

### （六）成本收入比指标

### (VI) Objectives of cost to income ratio

成本收入比持续控制在 35% 以内。

The ratio shall be always controlled as within 35%.

### （七）资本充足指标

## (VII) Capital adequacy objectives

资本充足率持续保持在 10% 以上,年内确保完成增资扩股,年末资本充足率达到 13.6%。

The adequacy shall be always kept above 10%. The share capital increase to be completed within the year shall increase the adequacy to 13.6% by the end of the year.

## （八）拔备和减值准备指标

## (VIII) Provision and impairment objectives

拨备覆盖率持续保持在 100% 以上,年末达到 200% 以上；贷款损失准备充足率持续保持在 100% 以上,年末达到 300% 以上；

The provision coverage shall be maintained over 100% and increased to over 200% by the end of the year; loan loss provision adequacy shall be maintained over 100% and increased to over 300% by the end of the year;

资产损失准备充足率持续保持在100%以上,年末达到200%以上。

Assets loss provision adequacy shall be maintained over 100% and increased to over 300% by the end of the year.

## （九）贷款质量指标

(IX) Loan quality objectives

按五级分类标准,不良贷款率持续控制在1.05%以下。

As per the five categories standard, ratio of non-performance loans shall be controlled below 1.05%.

## §10 监事会报告

#### §10 BOS Report

## 一、认真落实工作制度，较好地履行了工作职责

### I. Duly implementing roles and responsibilities

2010 年，监事会共召开了4次监事会会议，审议通过了本行2009年度审计报告、2009年度经营管理情况报告、对董事、监事2009年度履行职责评价报告、2009年度监事会工作报告及2010年度监事会工作意见等议案，完成了监事会规章制度的修订完善工作，听取了监事会审计委员会专项业务检查审计情况、高级管理层经营管理情况及监事会专项巡视情况等报告。

In 2010, BOS has held 4 BOS meetings for reviewing and adopting the Bank’s 2009 annual audit report, 2009 annual operation management report, 2009 performance evaluation report on Directors and Supervisors, 2009 BOS working report, and 2010 annual BOS working opinions. BOS also revised the BOS rules and systems, heard about BOS audit committee report on special business inspection and audit, performance of senior management, and report on BOS special tour inspection.

2010 年，监事会专门委员会共召开4次会议，对年度审计报告、专项业务审计报告、财务分析报告及有关报表资料等进行了审查，对监事会成员年度履职情况进行了考评，各专门委员会有效地协助监事会开展各项监督工作。

In 2010, BOS special committees have held 4 meetings for reviewing annual audit report, special business audit report, financial analysis report, and other reports and documents. The committees also evaluated annual performance of members of BOS. They provided effective support to the BOS in carrying out monitoring functions.

## 二、突出重点，认真做好监督检查工作

II. Targeted monitoring and inspection

1、加强财务监督，促进进一步提高财务管理水平

#### 1. Financial monitoring for further improving capacity in financial management

2010年，监事会密切关注本行定期报告以及相关财务核算的真实、准确和完整性。一是认真审查年度审计报告，对财务数据进行逐项核对，对信息披露的重要事项进行认真审查，确保年度审计报告真实、公允地反映年度经营成果。二是加强日常财务活动的监督，每月认真分析有关业务报表和财务分析报告，重点关注财务指标变动情况，对照监管指标和年度经营目标，对业务发展中出现的经营偏差和问题提出近40条建议。三是根据年度财务状况及日常财务变化情况，研究可能影响财务状况的风险因素，对有关情况提出改进建议。

In 2010, BOS has kept a close eye on the authenticity, accuracy, and completeness of the Bank's periodical reports and other financial accountings. The first is careful review of annual audit report and verification of financial data by items. The careful review of key issues disclosed in the information ensures that the annual audit report can truly and fairly represent annual operating results. The second is monitoring of daily financial activities and analyzing monthly operational report and financial analysis report with the focus on changes in financial indicators. In comparison with supervising indicators and annual operation objectives, nearly 40 recommendations are proposed for deviations and issues identified. The third is identification of risk factors that may affect financial position according to annual financial positions and daily financial changes for proposing recommendations.

#### 2、加强风险与内控监督，促进依法稳健经营

#### 2. Enhancing monitoring of risks and internal control to promoting sound and lawful operation

2010 年，监事会重点关注本行风险管理与内部控制情况，组织开展了了对部分支行授信业务风险控制专项审计，重点检查信用风险、市场风险、操作风险、内控制度执行情况等。在加强检查审计的同时，监事会对非信贷资产也进行了持续关注，并提出了加强管理的建议，推动强化对非信贷类资产的风险管理。

In 2010, BOS focused on risk control and internal control. Special audits on risk control were carried out on some branches credit businesses, in particular, including inspection of credit risks, market risks, operational risks, and implementation of internal control. In addition, BOS also kept a close eye on non-credit assets. The recommendations on strengthening management promoted risk management of these assets.

3、对董事会、高管层及成员履职情况进行监督评价，促进依法有效履职

3. Monitoring and assessment of performance of BOD, senior management, and their members to promote lawful and effective performance

2010 年，监事会列席了全部的董事会会议，指派人员列席了高级管理层会议，不定期查阅董事会及专门委员会的有关会议资料和记录，监督各项决策的合规合法性、落实股东大会决议和授权情况，从监督角度发表意见。在10月份监事会听取了高级管理层前三季度经营管理情况的报告，对照年度经营计划和监管要求发表了建议，督促落实年度经营计划。

In 2010, BOS attended all the BOD meetings, assigned members to participate in senior management meetings, examined irregularly related minutes and recordings of BOD and special committees’ meetings, monitored legal compliance of various decisions, implemented general meeting resolution and authorization, and prepared monitoring opinions. In October, BOS heard report by senior management on operation in the last three quarters. Recommendations for promoting implementation of annual operational plan were proposed according to annual operational plan and supervisory requirements.

监事会依照《监事会对董事履职评价办法》有关规定，对本行董事会成员2009年度履职情况进行了评价，形成了《关于对董事2009年度履行职责情况的评价报告》并报股东大会审议通过，对促进董事自觉履行职责发挥了积极作用。

As per related provisions in the “Performance Evaluation of Directors by BOS”, BOS evaluated the performance of BOD members in 2009. The “Evaluation Report on Directors Performance in 2009” reviewed and adopted by the general meeting played an active role in promoting active performance of duties of Directors.

## 三. 调研巡视情况

### III. Research and tour inspection

监事会在工作中注重开展调研巡视活动，并提出改善和加强管理的建议。

Based on extensive research and tour inspection activities, BOS proposed recommendations on improving and strengthening management.

在 2010 年 3 月份和 10 月份，监事会分别对青岛分行进行调研，对青岛分行以及异地开办分支机构在业务拓展及管理中的有关情况提出了建议。

In March and October of 2010, BOS carried out studies on Qingdao Branch. It proposed recommendations on the Branch and other local branches' role in business expansion and management.

5 月份，监事会针对当前细分客户、推行差异化发展战略以及个人业务发展情况开展巡视，形成《关于个人业务等有关情况的巡视报告》，对当前存在的问题提出建议。

In May, based on the strategies of market segmentation and differentiation, personal business development, and a tour inspection, BOS prepared the “Tour Report on Personal Business and Related

Issues", in which recommendations were proposed for handling issues identified.

11、12 月份，监事会分别两次召集部分支行负责人座谈，了解特色化经营情况，了解经营管理遇到的困难和政策执行中遇到的问题，交流工作经验和体会，根据存在的问题向董事会、高级管理层提出建议，对促进制度创新、方法创新和战略目标的实施发挥了积极作用。

In November and December, BOS held two informal discussions with some branches' head officer for understanding current situations in special operating activities, understanding difficulties and issues in management and policy implementation, exchanging experiences and understandings, and recommending to the BOD and senior management. These activities played an active role in promoting system innovation, method innovation, and implementing of strategic objectives.

第三、四季度，监事会分别组织监事对部分支行开展了机关效能工作情况的巡视，分季度调查了解总行机关工作质量、工作效率、服务水平等情况，查找问题和不足，听取意见和建议，对机关效能工作提出了改进意见。

In the third and fourth quarter, BOS has sent Supervisors to some branches for tour inspection of functioning of the Bank’s departments, including surveying of their work quality, efficiency, and service level. Improvement recommendations were proposed on functioning of departments based on issues identified and comments and recommendations collected.

四、完善工作关系与沟通模式，确保履职环境

IV. Improving working relationship and communication model for facilitating fulfillment of duties

根据《公司法》、《本行章程》等赋予监事会的职责，监事会以客观公正、积极务实的态度，与监管机构、各股东、董事会以及高级管理层成员保持了较为畅通的沟通渠道和工作关系，不断强化与有关各方的信息交流，以建设性的工作精神，支持董事会和高管层的工作；以维护本行、全体股东和存款人的根本利益为至高原则，注重监督与服务并重，审慎监督与介入的职责界限，在确保监事会履行职责的同时，在与有关各方建立起密切的、建设性的工作关系方面，为监督工作的顺利开展营造了良好的环境。

According to roles and responsibilities of the BOS specified in the “Corporate Law” and the “Articles of Association”, BOS adopted an objective and practical attitude in building sound communication channels and working relationship with regulators, shareholders, BOD, and senior management. The increased communication with related parties and aggressive working spirit facilitated the work of BOD and senior management. In safeguarding the fundamental interests of the Bank, shareholders, and depositors, BOS provided both monitoring and services. It was also careful about the

distinction between monitoring and interference. The close and constructive working relationship with various parties facilitated the monitoring work.

## 五、监事勤勉尽责情况

### V. Due diligence of Supervisors

2010，监事会全体成员按照规定按时参加股东大会，出席监事会会议和专门委员会会议，参加调研巡视，列席董事会会议，会议亲自出席率100%。各位监事认真研究会议议案，积极收集有关议案的背景资料，提出明确的意见和建议，认真阅读本行提供的各类文件报告，及时了解本行经营管理状况，认真、勤勉地履行了监督职责。

In 2010, all the members of BOS participated in the general meetings, BOS meetings, and special committees' meeting, research and tour inspection, and BOD meetings in person 100%. The Supervisors carefully studied meeting proposals, actively collected background information on proposals, and proposed clear opinions and recommendations. They also carefully read various documents and reports provided by the Bank to understand its operation and management conditions.

六、加强自身建设，提高监督能力

### VI. Capacity building for improving functioning of Supervisors

2010，监事会进一步加强了自身建设。一是加强监事会成员的日常学习积累，利用网络、报刊、文件、参加研讨班等多种形式学习有关金融知识、监管政策、规范性文件，学习交流上市公司监事会的工作经验和工作方法，不断丰富专业理论知识，监督工作水平有了进一步提高。二是按照发展战略和监管要求，在对监事会各项规章制度进行梳理、修订完善基础上，审议通过了《监事会巡视制度》、《监事会信息沟通制度》、《监事会对董事履职评价办法》等新制度，监事会的规章制度得到进一步完善。

In 2010, BOS furthered its capacity building program. The first is that BOS members are actively studying the financial knowledge, regulatory policies, specifications, working experiences and methods of BOS of listing companies though the internet, news and journals, documents, and seminars. These activities improved its skills in monitoring. The second is based on development strategies and regulatory requirements; BOS reviewed and adopted new systems such as “BOS Tour Inspection System”, “BOS Communication System”, “Evaluation of Directors’ Performance by BOS” for further improving rules and systems of the BOS.

## 七、监事会就有关事项发表的独立意见

VII. Independent opinions expressed by BOS

（一）公司依法运作情况。报告期内，本行的经营活动符合《公司法》、《商业银行法》和《公司章程》的规定，决策程序合法有效，未发现有违反法律、法规或损害本行及股东利益的行为。

(Ⅰ) Company’s compliance of laws: in the reporting period, operating activities of the Bank complied with provisions of the “Corporate Law”, “Commercial Bank Law”, and “Articles of Association”. The decision-making procedures are lawful and effective. No behavior in violation of laws, regulations, or the Bank and its shareholders’ interests has been identified.

（二）财务报告的真实性。山东正源和信有限责任会计师事务所潍坊分所对我行编制的2010年度财务报告进行了审计，并出具了标准无保留意见的审计报告，财务报告真实、公允地反映了本行财务状况和经营成果。

(II) Authenticity of financial reports: Weifang Branch, Shandong Zhengyuanhexin CPAs has audited the 2010 Annual Financial Report prepared by the Bank and issued a standard qualified audit report, indicating that the financial report is a true and fair representation of the Bank's financial position and operating results.

（三）关联交易情况。报告期内，本行关联交易符合商业原则，未发现损害公司和股东利益的行为。

(III) Affiliated transaction: in the reporting period, affiliated transactions of the Bank complied with commercial principals without any behavior in violation of the Company and its shareholders' interests.

（四）购置、出售资产情况。监事会没有发现在资产出售、购置中有内幕交易或损害股东权益的行为。

(IV) Procurement and disposal of assets: BOS identified no insider trading and other behavior in violation of shareholders' interests in the process.

（五）股东大会决议执行情况。报告期内，监事会对董事会提交股东大会审议的各项报告和议案没有异议。监事会对董事会执行股东大会决议的全过程进行了监督，认为董事会能够认真执行股东大会的有关决议。

(V) Implementing of resolutions of the general meeting: in the reporting period, BOS has no objection to reports and proposals submitted to the general meeting by BOD. Through monitoring the whole process in implementing resolutions of the general meeting by BOD, BOS believed that BOD duly implemented those resolutions.

（六）内部控制及战略规划情况。监事会同意董事会对本行内部控制完整性、合理性、有效性及内控制度执行情况的说明，同意董事会制定的全行发展战略规划。

(VI) Internal control and strategic planning: BOS agreed with BOD in its description of the completeness, appropriateness, effectiveness, and implementation of internal control. It also agreed with BOD on the Bank’s development strategic planning prepared.

## §11 重要事项

#### §11 Important Issues

## （一）重要诉讼事项

## (Ⅰ) Important lawsuits

2007年3月1日，本行为临沂富安经贸有限公司贴现了8张1000万元共计8000万元的银行承兑汇票，其中4张承兑银行为中信银行石家庄分行，另外4张承兑银行为交通银行石家庄分行，到期日期均为2007年6月6日。2007年3月5日，本行与北京银行达成转贴现协议，将上述8000万元票据转贴现给北京银行。2007年6月4日，本行与北京银行签署转贴现协议，受让其中承兑银行为交通银行石家庄分行的4张票据。2007年5月30日，中信银行石家庄分行以票据丢失为由向石家庄市桥西区人民法院申请公示催告，本行于2007年6月12日向法院主张权利。2007年6月13日，中信银行石家庄分行以本行不享有票据权利为由将本行石家庄市中级人民法院，起诉额为8000万元。本行于2007年8月14日以中信银行石家庄分行、交通银行石家庄分行不兑付到期票据为由分两笔（每笔4000万元）将其诉至潍坊市中级人民法院。

On March 1, 2007, the Bank accepted the total amount of RMB 80 million bank acceptances, each of which has a face value of RMB 10 million, from Linyi Fuan Trading Co., Ltd. Accepting bank for 4 of the acceptances is Shijiazhuang Branch, CITIC Bank while the other 4 is to be accepted by Shijiazhuang Branch, Bank of Communications. These acceptances were to be matured on June 6, 2007. On March 5, 2007, the Bank transferred them of RMB 80 million to Bank of Beijing according to a transfer discount agreement reached. On June 4, 2007, the Bank accepted 4 of these acceptances to be accepted by Shijiazhuang Branch, Bank of Communications according to a transfer discount agreement reached with the Bank of Beijing. On May 30, 2007, Shijiazhuang Branch, CITIC Bank filed a public notice with Shijiazhuang Qiaoxi District Court based on the ground that it has lost the acceptance. The Bank claimed with the Court on June 12, 2007. On June 13, 2007, Shijiazhuang Branch, CITIC Bank filed a lawsuit against the Bank for RMB 80 million based on the ground that the Bank has no rights over the acceptance. The Bank filed a lawsuit on August 14, 2007 with Weifang Intermediary People's Court.

on the ground that Shijiazhuang Branch, CITIC Bank and Shijiazhuang Branch, Bank of Communications failed to accept the two sums of acceptances (each worth RMB 40 million).

截止2010年12月31日，本行挂账暂付北京银行4000万元的票据款以及交通银行石家庄分行到期未解付4000万元的票据。2011年1月，根据民事调解书和本行与中信银行石家庄分行签订的和解协议书，上述款项已全部收回。

As at December 31, 2010, the Bank recorded RMB 40 million provisional payment for the acceptance to Bank of Beijing and acceptance of RMB 40 million overdue by Shijiazhuang Branch, Bank of Communications. In January 2011, according to civil mediation and settlement agreement reached with Shijiazhuang Branch, CITIC Bank, the money has been totally collected.

## （二）报告期内发生的重大关联交易事项

##### (II) Important affiliated transactions in the reporting period

报告期内，本行一直坚持诚信、公允的商业原则，对所有关联方贷款均按国家相关法律规定、以及本行贷款条件和审核程序发放。现有的关联交易对本行正常经营活动不会产生重大实质影响。

Within the reporting period, the Bank carries out the business policies of integrity and equality, all the loans to related parties are distributed as per relevant laws and regulations of the state, as well as conditions and review procedures of the Bank. The current affiliated transactions will not exert substantial and material influences on the normal activities of the Bank.
